import { BlockInfoCard } from "@/components/ui/block-info-card"

<BlockInfoCard type="rootly" color="#6C72C8" />

Instructions d'utilisation [#instructions-dutilisation]

Integrate Rootly incident management into workflows. Create and manage incidents, alerts, services, severities, and retrospectives.

Outils [#outils]

rootly_create_incident [#rootly_create_incident]

Create a new incident in Rootly with optional severity, services, and teams.

Entrée [#entrée]

| Paramètre          | Type    | Obligatoire | Description                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------ | ------- | ----------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`           | string  | Oui         | Rootly API key                                                                                                                            |
| `title`            | string  | Non         | The title of the incident (auto-generated if not provided)                                                                                |
| `summary`          | string  | Non         | A summary of the incident                                                                                                                 |
| `severityId`       | string  | Non         | Severity ID to attach to the incident                                                                                                     |
| `status`           | string  | Non         | Incident status (in\_triage, started, detected, acknowledged, mitigated, resolved, closed, cancelled, scheduled, in\_progress, completed) |
| `kind`             | string  | Non         | Incident kind (normal, normal\_sub, test, test\_sub, example, example\_sub, backfilled, scheduled, scheduled\_sub)                        |
| `serviceIds`       | string  | Non         | Comma-separated service IDs to attach                                                                                                     |
| `environmentIds`   | string  | Non         | Comma-separated environment IDs to attach                                                                                                 |
| `groupIds`         | string  | Non         | Comma-separated team/group IDs to attach                                                                                                  |
| `incidentTypeIds`  | string  | Non         | Comma-separated incident type IDs to attach                                                                                               |
| `functionalityIds` | string  | Non         | Comma-separated functionality IDs to attach                                                                                               |
| `labels`           | string  | Non         | Labels as JSON object, e.g. \{"platform":"osx","version":"1.29"}                                                                          |
| `private`          | boolean | Non         | Create as a private incident (cannot be undone)                                                                                           |

Sortie [#sortie]

| Paramètre        | Type    | Description                     |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------- |
| `incident`       | object  | The created incident            |
| ↳ `id`           | string  | Unique incident ID              |
| ↳ `sequentialId` | number  | Sequential incident number      |
| ↳ `title`        | string  | Incident title                  |

rootly_snooze_alert [#rootly_snooze_alert]

Snooze a Rootly alert for a set number of minutes.

Entrée [#entrée-36]

| Paramètre      | Type   | Obligatoire | Description                           |
| -------------- | ------ | ----------- | ------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`       | string | Oui         | Rootly API key                        |
| `alertId`      | string | Oui         | The ID of the alert to snooze         |
| `delayMinutes` | number | Oui         | Number of minutes to snooze the alert |

Sortie [#sortie-36]

| Paramètre            | Type   | Description       |
| -------------------- | ------ | ----------------- |
| `alert`              | object | The snoozed alert |
| ↳ `id`               | string | Unique alert ID   |
| ↳ `shortId`          | string | Short alert ID    |
| ↳ `summary`          | string | Alert summary     |
| ↳ `description`      | string | Alert description |
| ↳ `source`           | string | Alert source      |
| ↳ `status`           | string | Alert status      |
| ↳ `externalId`       | string | External ID       |
| ↳ `externalUrl`      | string | External URL      |
| ↳ `deduplicationKey` | string | Deduplication key |
| ↳ `createdAt`        | string | Creation date     |
| ↳ `updatedAt`        | string | Last update date  |
| ↳ `startedAt`        | string | Start date        |
| ↳ `endedAt`          | string | End date          |

rootly_list_incident_events [#rootly_list_incident_events]

List the timeline events for a Rootly incident.

Entrée [#entrée-38]

| Paramètre    | Type   | Obligatoire | Description                            |
| ------------ | ------ | ----------- | -------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`     | string | Oui         | Rootly API key                         |
| `incidentId` | string | Oui         | The ID of the incident                 |
| `pageSize`   | number | Non         | Number of items per page (default: 20) |
| `pageNumber` | number | Non         | Page number for pagination             |

Sortie [#sortie-38]

| Paramètre      | Type   | Description                             |
| -------------- | ------ | --------------------------------------- |
| `events`       | array  | List of incident timeline events        |
| ↳ `id`         | string | Unique event ID                         |
| ↳ `event`      | string | The event description                   |
| ↳ `visibility` | string | Event visibility (internal or external) |
| ↳ `occurredAt` | string | When the event occurred                 |
| ↳ `createdAt`  | string | Creation date                           |
| ↳ `updatedAt`  | string | Last update date                        |
| `totalCount`   | number | Total number of events returned         |

rootly_run_workflow [#rootly_run_workflow]

Trigger a Rootly automation workflow, optionally scoped to an incident or alert.

Entrée [#entrée-39]

| Paramètre         | Type    | Obligatoire | Description                                                                    |
| ----------------- | ------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `apiKey`          | string  | Oui         | Rootly API key                                                                 |
| `workflowId`      | string  | Oui         | The ID of the workflow to run                                                  |
| `incidentId`      | string  | Non         | Incident ID to run the workflow against                                        |
| `alertId`         | string  | Non         | Alert ID to run the workflow against                                           |
| `immediate`       | boolean | Non         | Run immediately (true) or respect the workflow wait time (false). Default true |
| `checkConditions` | boolean | Non         | Whether to evaluate the workflow conditions before running. Default false      |

Sortie [#sortie-39]

| Paramètre         | Type   | Description                                                                        |
| ----------------- | ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `workflowRun`     | object | The triggered workflow run                                                         |
| ↳ `id`            | string | Unique workflow run ID                                                             |
| ↳ `workflowId`    | string | ID of the workflow that ran                                                        |
| ↳ `status`        | string | Run status (queued, started, completed, completed\_with\_errors, failed, canceled) |
| ↳ `statusMessage` | string | Status detail message                                                              |
| ↳ `triggeredBy`   | string | What triggered the run (system, user, workflow)                                    |
| ↳ `incidentId`    | string | Associated incident ID                                                             |
| ↳ `alertId`       | string | Associated alert ID                                                                |
| ↳ `startedAt`     | string | When the run started                                                               |
| ↳ `completedAt`   | string | When the run completed                                                             |
| ↳ `failedAt`      | string | When the run failed                                                                |
| ↳ `canceledAt`    | string | When the run was canceled                                                          |
